    SELECT TOP(1) [m].[Id], [m].[AgeRating], [m].[Country], [m].[Description], [m].[Duration], [m].[GenreId], [m].[Language], [m].[Poster], [m].[ProducerId], [m].[Rating], [m].[Title], [m].[VideoPath], [m].[Year], [p].[Id] AS [Id0], [p].[Bio], [p].[Image], [p].[Name], [g].[Id] AS [Id1], [g].[Description] AS [Description0], [g].[Icon], [g].[Name] AS [Name0], [g].[NameAr]
    FROM [Movies] AS [m]
    INNER JOIN [Producers] AS [p] ON [m].[ProducerId] = [p].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [Genres] AS [g] ON [m].[GenreId] = [g].[Id]
    WHERE [m].[Id] = @__id_0
) AS [s]

    SELECT [a].[ActorId], [a].[MovieId], [a0].[Id], [a0].[Bio], [a0].[Image], [a0].[Name]

) AS [s0] ON [s].[Id] = [s0].[MovieId]
LEFT JOIN [MovieQualities] AS [m0] ON [s].[Id] = [m0].[MovieId]
